== SUMMARY OF WHAT WAS REMOVED ==
- madgraph/MG5_aMC_v3.4.0.tar.gz (27M) and pythia/pythia8307.tgz (25M): stock release tarballs, can be downloaded again
- madgraph/MG5_aMC_v3_4_0/: stock MadGraph5_aMC@NLO 3.4.0 install (tests, Template, vendor, models, sources, __pycache__).
    The only stock file that differed from the tarball was input/.autoupdate (an update timestamp).
    From MY_FIRST_MG5_RUN (a test run with no events generated), only the three cards that define the run were kept.
- pythia/pythia8307/: stock Pythia 8.307 install (src, include, share docs/xmldoc, plugins, lib, tmp build objects).
    Kept: your own example sources (.cc), .cmnd files, and text outputs. examples/main80.cmnd was the only stock file you had edited.
    Removed: compiled Mach-O binaries (main01, main80, maininput01, mymain*, mymainextended0*), which can be rebuilt from the .cc files.
- All .DS_Store files and the LibreOffice lock file 'Monte Carlo jobs/.~lock.updates.odt#'
